# Results registry — every citable number, and the conditions that produced it

Curated 2026-09-11 by CURATE (read-only; no number here was re-measured). Companion files:
`.scratch/DOCUMENT-STATUS.md` (which documents are in force) and
`.scratch/curation/PROVENANCE-HEADER.md` (the block every new report must carry).

> Owner instruction this file serves: *"要小心不要把舊專案的一些結果混進來了，新專案本身的很多結果也是基於
> 不同的條件生成的，全部都要備註清楚不能誤用."* A number here is a property of the procedure that produced
> it. Before comparing two rows, check §3.

**How the rows were built.** Each row records what its source document *says*. Where two documents
disagree about a number's conditions, both are listed and the row says `CONFLICT`. Rows marked with
prefixes `AP/CF/RH/DQ/LF/DR/RV/TD/DS` (grounding, reviews, deltas), `CS/ZC/ZT/CH/SC` (catfish-surface,
zclose, zscore-transfer, concept-harvest, catfish-screens) and `SV` (V0.25 server reports, read over
read-only ssh) were extracted by three read-only extraction passes and spot-checked; `FF/BP/B0/PA/CP/EM/CT`
were written directly from the reports and controller documents. Several rows bundle 2–6 values that
share every condition. `UNKNOWN` means the source does not say.

**Added 2026-09-12 by CURATE-2 (§5).** Prefixes `H4-` (H4 probe), `LP-` (LP grid), `OR-` (oracle cells),
`EC-` (EE ceiling), `T0R-` (T0 representability), `TSQ-` (T_SEQ representability / B2), `B1C-` (B1 credit) and
`E0-` (E0 development) were extracted by reading each family's source document end to end and transcribing what
it states, with a `file:line` citation per row; nothing was re-measured, recomputed or reconciled, and where a
source is silent the cell reads `UNKNOWN`. Where two in-force documents state different things about the same
quantity, both are listed and the row says `CONFLICT`. **Revised 2026-09-12 after
`.scratch/curation/CONTROLLER-Q9B-ADJUDICATION-2026-09-12.md`:** the MDP-modifier UNKNOWNs and the `E0-`
provenance UNKNOWNs are now resolved from controller-verified facts (see the §5 preamble conditions 3 and 5 and
the §5 revision note at the end of the file), OR-17 is `CORRECTED` rather than `CONFLICT`, and OR-19 is the only
`CONFLICT` left. Condition text shared by a whole family sits in that section's preamble and its rows say `as <ID>` or
`as the preamble`; several rows bundle a whole table of values that share every condition. **E1 is deliberately
not curated** — its runs were still going when §5 was written. Before appending, each family's headline values
and commits were grepped against the existing file: none appeared, so **no pre-existing row is superseded by
§5**.

---

## 0. Read this first — the seven condition traps

1. **Two TLE archives on the MODQN harness (B0 ruling §4; `.scratch/b0-corrected/PROGRESS.md:1-17`).**
   - **Pinned** archive: `file_set_sha256 427e6a91774b0ebf3d9b5a13dd783fdaa3f107666f9e9a6cb2a08d5c92b38fe9`
     (373 files, 2025-07-27…2026-08-20; pin commit `b924c8a0`; == the frozen R2 prereg's ephemeris hash).
     Placebo signature: **RANDOM_MASKED = 52,420,510.0956937 bit/J** (24 ep, seeds 42/1337/7), bit-identical
     on local and sat. The frozen checkpoint `e6b063ef…` and its `episode-logs.json` were produced on this
     archive (sat's default before the pin; same content).
   - **Unpinned** local archive `~/demo/tle_data/starlink/tle`, `file_set_sha256 e07f3e1e879dafd2…093e4`
     (392 files). Placebo signature: **RANDOM_MASKED = 53,060,175.56 bit/J**. The catfish-surface harness and
     **everything built on it — FEASFRONT, CFSCREEN, the anchor-ablation and beam-power-accounting runs, the
     EEGAP bridge, the pooled-EE rulings** — ran here.
   - **These two groups are NOT-COMPARABLE to each other.** In the tables the host column reads
     `local, unpinned e07f3e1e…` / `L-unpinned` vs `sat, pinned 427e6a91…` / `S-frozen` (sat before the pin;
     same content as the pin) / `S-2026-08-25` (the frozen run's own training logs; pinned content).
   - **HOST DOES NOT IMPLY ARCHIVE — corrected 2026-09-12** (`.scratch/curation/CONTROLLER-Q9B-ADJUDICATION-2026-09-12.md`
     §5). The three bullets above describe how the two archives *happened* to be distributed across hosts up to
     2026-09-11; they are **not a mapping**. There is a **pinned copy on the local host** at
     `/home/u24/mcrl-runtime/tle-pinned-427e6a91`, and the B1 credit lane (rows `B1C-`, §5g) ran on the local
     WSL2 laptop **on that pinned copy** — reproducing the sat pinned reference rolls bit-for-bit on every
     published field (B1C-01). **Only `file_set_sha256` decides comparability.** Never classify a number by its
     host: read the archive hash in the row's host cell, and if a row states a host without an archive hash,
     treat the archive as UNKNOWN rather than guessing it from the host.
2. **Same seeds ≠ same episodes (CFSCREEN; `V025-CONTROLLER-RECORD-CATFISH-SCREENS-2026-09-11.md:47-52`).**
   On the MODQN harness only **episode 0** is paired across cells; for episodes ≥ 1 the start epoch comes from
   `env_rng` and the number of fading draws depends on actions, so t=0 SNR/θ differ in 100 % of rows. Every
   between-arm difference in FEASFRONT, CATFISHSURFACE, POWERACCT, the anchor ablation and B0 is therefore an
   **unpaired** comparison (unpaired per-episode sems are valid; **any paired statistic across cells is
   INVALID-AS-PAIRED** — rows CS-33 and B0-12). Within-run re-pricings (POWERACCT's three accountings of one
   recorded run; same-state rule queries such as SC-09) *are* paired. The P6-scenario harness used on sat
   (MODQN-COLLAPSE, MODQNZ, zclose) re-seeds each scenario; whether its cells are paired was not checked.
3. **Shared env vs fresh env (B0 R.5).** The original catfish-surface driver ran every arm on one
   environment; `_age_rng` carries across episodes, so only the first arm (RANDOM) ran at age-stream
   positions 0–23. Hence **two "trained EE" values circulate**: 93,137,893.02 (shared env, arm-order
   affected; CS-02) and **93,110,907.97** (fresh env; CS-16 = FF-04 = BP-01 = EM bridge). Cite with the round.
4. **Physics / harness vocabulary.** `MODQN-harness` = the `src/mcrl/env` step environment the frozen
   checkpoint was trained in (dt 30.08 s, 100 users, 10 steps/episode, 28 user-relative actions, per-beam
   power = **max over served users**, consumed = PA supply + 0.338 W/beam + 0.200 W/active sat, no cap, no
   rate target). `V025-a-r0-panel` = the V0.25 successor engine, cell `a-r0`, interruption off, full-buffer,
   **time-division (TDM) per-beam accounting**, global `(norad_id, cell_id)` options with an explicit capped
   active set; panels: **12-anchor dev** (`V025_PROBE/world/1` steps 0–3 × 3 carriers; effective n = 4
   steps), **93-anchor**, **22 TRAIN**, **20-anchor scoring** (all 20 are training anchors — in-sample,
   erratum 21). `V025 rate-target probe panel` = the 20-/8-anchor and 30-date panels of the morning of
   2026-09-10 (sealed vs corrected provisioning). `V0.23` = the predecessor engine (C3-S, S0).
   `SIBLING-familyb-JULY` / `SIBLING-post-2026-08-05` = the old project (§2). The two harnesses share no
   action or observation space (erratum 23 §4; SV-BR-01).
5. **Estimand vocabulary.** `pooled ratio-of-sums` = Σ bits / Σ joules, divided once (the declared EE);
   `mean-of-ratios` = mean over steps/episodes of per-step EE (e.g. `r1_mean`); `per-user mean` = the
   sibling's `argmax_EE`; `trained calibrated scalar` = Σ ω_j r_j / c_j with ω (0.5, 0.3, 0.2), c
   (2029238.43, 1, 6) — **tree-dependent** (the D-2 per-step outage floor `c00aca3e` shifts it by
   −0.005…−0.016); `uncalibrated 0.5·r1` = the frozen run's logged `scalar_reward` (r2, r3 move it by 1e-6);
   counts (beams, slots, users) are never EEs — **G-3 `active_beam_count` is distinct relative slots out of
   28, not physical beams** (7.47 vs 67.90 physical for the same checkpoint).
6. **CF3 pilot episode sets (2026-09-11; `P3-` rows, §4x).** The CF3 pilot's evaluation set
   (`env=9,111,000+i` / `mobility=9,112,000+i`, i=0..23) vs its calibration set (`9,121,000+i` /
   `9,122,000+i`) vs the frozen-run stream seeds 42/1337/7 are **three different episode sets**; the
   premeasure block (`cf3-pilot/PROGRESS.md:48-62`) is on the calibration set. The pilot's learning-speed
   and eta-trajectory readings (P3-32..44) are calibration-set numbers and are **NOT comparable to** its
   final-checkpoint / pairwise / C-S rows (P3-01..31), which are evaluation-set numbers.

7. **No pre-S1 value of the frozen checkpoint `e6b063ef…` is citable as the baseline** (ruling, 2026-09-12;
   `.scratch/curation/CONTROLLER-Q9B-ADJUDICATION-2026-09-12.md` §6, binding via Amendment 13 §2). Six mutually
   incomparable pooled-EE values **measured from that checkpoint** are now in this file — **89,911,720** (EC-01),
   **93,110,907.97** (FF-04 = CS-16 = BP-01), **93,137,893.02** (DR-03 = CS-02), **93,787,980.20** (FF-05),
   **93,902,816.57** (EC-02) and **94,413,179** (E0-02) — across different episode sets, archives, hosts and
   harness variants. The adjudication enumerates five values; EC-01 and EC-02 were curated after it was written
   and fall under the same rule, and its fifth entry **90,866,329.62 (SV-MZ-01) is by its own source a
   from-scratch MODQN_RAW retrain with the frozen checkpoint only hashed**, not a measurement of `e6b063ef…` —
   equally not citable, but not one of the six. Further values for the same checkpoint exist under **non-default
   accountings and ablated physics** (BP-02 94,111,458.32 TDM, BP-03 80,465,428.82 ADDITIVE, CS-14 92,130,894.38
   `ablate_anchor`); they are labelled as such in their own rows and are covered by this trap too. **The only
   authoritative figure for the published 9,000-episode reference is the S1 rollout of `e6b063ef…` on the formal
   evaluation set under the same protocol as the six trained arms**, which does not exist yet. Every row carrying
   one of these values keeps it as a **historical measurement with its conditions attached** and is marked
   `NOT CITABLE AS THE BASELINE (pre-S1)`. If the S1 rollout lands materially away from all of them, that is
   expected — they were measured under different conditions — and is not evidence of a problem.

Other conditions every MODQN-harness row must state: tree flags (bootstrap `eq16-per-head-max` vs
`shared-continuation` — commit `5219995a` had it unconditional, `57fb40b4` made it a flag defaulting to
eq. 16; D-2 floor off / −100 / per-step; D-3; cap flag), checkpoint (frozen 9,000-ep `e6b063ef…` vs a
500-ep retrain at training ε 0.753), and whether the number is a greedy evaluation or a training-time log.

## 2. Old-project (sibling) numbers — **REFERENCE-ONLY — NOT COMPARABLE** — 61 rows

Why none of these may be compared with any number in §1 (per `.scratch/ee-magnitude/EE-MAGNITUDE-RECONCILIATION-2026-09-11.md` and erratum 28):

- **Estimand.** The cited July numbers (2026-07-11 … 07-21; 146–620) are the sibling's `argmax_EE`: a mean over users → steps →
  episodes of η_u = R_u·N_b/P_b (rev `0082683d`, `family_b_recalibration.py:121-167`), unserved users counting as zero, /1e6.
  Numbers produced after commit `60807490` (2026-08-05) use system EE ÷ U — a third scale (~1/100 of pooled consumed EE).
  Tag: `SIBLING-familyb-JULY` vs `SIBLING-post-2026-08-05`.
- **Denominator.** Radiated RF power only (no PA supply, no circuit, no baseband). On this project's own rollouts consumed
  power is **7.60×** radiated (EM-01).
- **Environment.** `k_cap = 3` beams **per window satellite** (≤ 12 beams; cap-bumped users score 0), G0 40 dBi, different
  noise, Walker-180 constellation, concave load-dependent RF power; this project has no cap, TLE constellation, 33 dBi.
- **Learning rate.** The collapsed "baseline MODQN" (~146–150) ran at lr 0.01; at lr 1e-3 the sibling baseline reads
  428.60 (6 seeds) / 493.18 (3 seeds). Several sibling negatives also used a "best checkpoint" selected on the uncalibrated
  scalar (CH-17).
- **Code era.** The sibling itself marked the capacity-penalty results as predating later fixes and invalid for its current
  protocol (CP report §1; CH-27).
- The only bridge is a **formula** bridge (EM-03: this project's trained checkpoint reads 693.87 on the sibling's July formula,
  random 373.61) — not a ranking of the two projects.

## 3. Comparability — which columns must match

Two numbers may be compared (subtracted, ratioed, called "consistent", ranked, or placed on one axis) only
if **all** of the following match. A mismatch on any line makes the comparison cross-condition; it must
then be labelled as such in the same sentence, or not made.

| column | must match | examples of a mismatch this project has already paid for |
|---|---|---|
| **quantity** | same quantity (EE with EE; not EE with a beam count, a win rate, a trained scalar) | 62.502712 Mbit/J vs 41.28 beams (erratum 23) |
| **physics / harness** (+ MDP modifiers) | same engine and panel; same cap / anchor / interruption treatment | V0.25 slope −425,009.885 applied to MODQN (erratum 28); capped vs uncapped MDP (CP-01) |
| **evaluation panel** | same anchors/episodes/seeds, same in-sample status; for V0.25, same boundary-0 vs full-48 scoring and same evaluator path | 12-anchor vs 93-anchor (62.502712 vs 62.5081); contaminated vs clean path (13.430253 vs 31.028111) |
| **estimand** | same estimator (ratio-of-sums vs mean-of-ratios vs per-user mean vs trained scalar) and same numerator (full-buffer vs demand-capped) | `r1_mean` +23.9 % read as EE (NO-DEMONSTRATOR "estimand defect"); demand-capped relabelling (RETRACTION-2) |
| **power accounting** | same denominator (consumed max / TDM_AIRTIME / radiated-only) | sibling radiated-only ×7.60 (EE-MAGNITUDE) |
| **host + TLE archive** | same archive hash; for the MODQN harness either both `e07f3e1e…` or both `427e6a91…` (placebo row matching) | sat CAPPENALTY vs local FEASFRONT (erratum 28:39-40) |
| **tree / flags** | same bootstrap mode, D-2 floor, D-3, penalty, z | FEASFRONT scalars (pre-D-2) vs CFSCREEN scalars (post-`c00aca3e`) |
| **policy / checkpoint** | same checkpoint class (frozen 9,000-ep vs 500-ep retrain vs training-time log at ε > 0); rule vs search winner | trained last-100 training episodes (ε≈0.01) vs greedy frozen-seed eval |
| **pairing** | a paired statistic requires cells that share episodes, not only seeds | CS-33, B0-12 |

A comparison that differs only in `n` is allowed if both sems are stated. A comparison across groups may be
shown **side by side** with the mismatch named (e.g. EE-MAGNITUDE's bridge, C1VSGAIN's "coincidence" note) —
those are listed in §4c as correctly handled.

### 4e. The three worst

1. **§4a #1–3 — 62.502712 Mbit/J vs 41.28.** An EE subtracted from a mean active-beam count, across two
   V0.25 panels, with a boundary-0 search winner labelled as a rule — and in the two-arm declaration offered
   as a demonstrator for a different physics. Withdrawn by reference (erratum 23) but still verbatim in two
   in-force 09-11 documents and in the DQfD grounding report.
2. **§4a #4–5 — the "no demonstrator on the trained objective" comparison.** Rule arms scored greedily on
   the local **unpinned** archive at stream positions 0–23, against the frozen run's own **training-time**
   last-100 episodes (ε≈0.01, positions 8900–8999) produced on the **pinned** archive, with `r1_mean`
   (a mean of ratios) read as EE. Host, archive, policy mode, episodes and estimand all differ; it carried
   the closure that erratum 27 later withdrew for other reasons, and it is still quoted in erratum 23 and
   the per-head-bootstrap finding. (Same family: #8 — erratum 28 corroborates sat CAPPENALTY with local
   FEASFRONT across host, archive, checkpoint and MDP; #11–12 — paired statistics across unpaired cells.)
3. **§4a #6–7 — the V0.25 beam slope −425,009.885 bit/J used on the MODQN harness** (erratum 24's "2.2×"
   consistency check and erratum 26's CA-CPBR sign argument), withdrawn by erratum 28 but unamended in both
   errata and in the zclose report.

## 5. Probes, screens, ceilings and development results of 2026-09-11/12 — all on the **pinned** archive `427e6a91…`, all on **new episode sets**

Curated 2026-09-12 by CURATE-2 (read-only; no number here was re-measured). Eight families, one section and one
ID prefix each: `H4-` (H4 probe), `LP-` (LP grid), `OR-` (oracle cells), `EC-` (EE ceiling), `T0R-` (T0
representability), `TSQ-` (T_SEQ representability, B2), `B1C-` (B1 credit), `E0-` (E0 development). **E1 is not
curated here** — its runs were still going; the controller adds those rows after the ep-300 readout.

Five conditions apply to **every** row of §5 and are the reason these rows must not be mixed with §1a/§1b/§1d:

1. **Archive.** Every family asserts the **pinned** file-set sha256
   `427e6a91774b0ebf3d9b5a13dd783fdaa3f107666f9e9a6cb2a08d5c92b38fe9` in every process. Nothing in §5 is on the
   unpinned `e07f3e1e…` archive, so **no §5 row may be compared with any §1a / L-unpinned row** (§0 trap 1).
2. **Host — and host does not imply archive (§0 trap 1, corrected 2026-09-12).** `sat` for every family **except
   `B1C-`**, which ran on the **local WSL2 laptop (`LAPTOP-O8M86FNI`) on the local pinned copy
   `/home/u24/mcrl-runtime/tle-pinned-427e6a91`**. Its placebo row (B1C-01) is the tie: `A m=2dB` re-rolled
   locally reproduces the sat pinned reference rolls on every published field. Classify these rows by the archive
   hash, never by the host.
3. **Episode sets — four of them, never comparable with each other.** `EVALUATION` = env `9,111,000+i` /
   mobility `9,112,000+i`, i = 0..23; `CALIBRATION` = env `9,121,000+i` / mobility `9,122,000+i`, i = 0..23;
   T0R-'s **training-like collection** = the pilot trainer's own draw from triples (42,1337,7) / (43,1338,8) /
   (44,1339,9); and the E0 lane's **DEVVAL** set = env `9,211,000+i` / mobility `9,212,000+i`, i = 0..23,
   24 episodes (verified by the controller from `runs-e1-d0-d3/E0-1-D0-equal_share-k3/devval-ep00300.json` on
   `sat` and ruled into force —
   `.scratch/curation/CONTROLLER-Q9B-ADJUDICATION-2026-09-12.md` §3; the seeds are now also written into the
   PROVENANCE block of `.scratch/dev-training/E0-BATCH-1-2026-09-12.md`, which had none until 2026-09-12).
   The pinned-archive placebo signature of §0 trap 1 (RANDOM_MASKED 52,420,510.0956937 on stream 42/1337/7) is
   **not** the placebo of these sets: on the calibration set RANDOM_MASKED is 51,866,475.48532767 (H4-01, EC-02),
   on the evaluation set 51,465,157 (EC-01), and on DEVVAL the RANDOM arm is 51,998,570 (E0-01). Same archive,
   different episodes, different number.
4. **Paired vs unpaired.** Unlike §0 trap 2's MODQN-harness cells, every family here reseeds **per episode**
   (`fresh env per episode`, seeds declared per episode index), so arms on the same set meet episode *i* at the
   same start epoch and the reports' `paired per-episode mean ± sem (wins/24)` statistics are paired **by
   construction within a set**. They are still never paired **across** sets.
5. **MDP modifiers — resolved 2026-09-12, formerly UNKNOWN in ~110 cells.** Every §5 family ran on commit
   `102b2d4d` or on `05aadf1b`, and the modifiers are properties of the **tree**, not of a run:
   `git merge-base --is-ancestor 102b2d4d 05aadf1b` is **true**, and
   `git diff --name-only 102b2d4d 05aadf1b -- src/` touches only
   `algorithms/{cf_credit,cf_dev,cf_ratio,cf_teacher}.py` — **no `env/` file changed between the two commits**, so
   the modifiers are byte-identical across both. Every `H4- LP- OR- EC- T0R- TSQ- B1C- E0-` row therefore carries
   **cap off / segment anchor on (`SEGMENT_START_POWER_W`, warm start "uniform-episode-length", not ablated) /
   interruption off / users 100**, and a physics cell reading `cap off / segment anchor on / interruption off
   (§5 condition 5)` means exactly that. Authority for the transfer is
   `.scratch/curation/CONTROLLER-Q9B-ADJUDICATION-2026-09-12.md` §4 plus the two git facts above — **not**
   `EE-CEILING-2026-09-11.md:7-9`, which is where the values were first stated for `102b2d4d` but which cannot by
   itself license the transfer to the other families. The same ruling covers E1.

Status wording used in §5: `SCREEN — continue-eligibility only (Amendment 4); not a paper claim` for results read
as Amendment 4 gate readings (`T0R-`, `TSQ-`, the `B1C-` screen cells and the `OR-` gate-reading rows);
`DEVELOPMENT — DEV/DEVVAL namespaces, not formal evidence, no confidence claim (Amendment 6)` for every `E0-` row;
and, for the probes/ceilings/measurement cells (`H4-`, `LP-`, `EC-`, the `OR-` measurement cells and the `B1C-`
engineering rows), the phrasing **each source document uses for its own standing**, quoted.

### 5b. LP grid — the two-parameter myopic score family LP(c, m) (`.scratch/h4-probe/LP-PROBE-2026-09-11.md`) — 27 rows

Source: `.scratch/h4-probe/LP-PROBE-2026-09-11.md`. Conditions common to every `LP-` row unless a cell says
otherwise: MODQN-harness, users 100, **cap off / segment anchor on / interruption off** (§5 condition 5; the
report's own words were "UNKNOWN", resolved 2026-09-12 from the tree by controller ruling);
pooled ratio-of-sums Σbits/ΣJ divided once, full-buffer Shannon, no demand cap; every "% vs `A m=2dB`" is the
pooled relative difference `ee_cell/ee_rule − 1` **against the rule on the same episode set**, and the statistic
beside it is the paired mean ± sem of the 24 per-episode relative differences (sem = sd(ddof=1)/√24) — an
uncertainty statistic, not the estimator; consumed, per-beam max over served users; host `sat`, pinned archive
`427e6a91…` asserted in every process; tree `mcrl-leo-handover-cf3`, branch `cf3/pilot-20260911`, commit
`102b2d4d`, with `scripts/lp_common.py` sha256 `fc8d4f0b…4a27` and `scripts/lp_grid.py` `b3a011f5…ecc5` — **LP-prev
and LP-seq are rules defined by this probe, not part of the frozen pilot code**; fresh env per episode, greedy
masked argmax, first index on ties, no rule consumes RNG; no trained network is touched; n = 24 episodes per cell
per set (96 rollouts + 2 reference rolls); `LP-prev` uses the previous-step `beam_loads` (simultaneous),
`LP-seq` decides users in fixed order 0..99 with the beam key read from `slot_tables[u]`.

### 5c. Oracle cells — A-real and B-real, rate-floored and unfloored (`.scratch/h4-probe/ORACLE-CELLS-2026-09-11.md` + two provisional controller files) — 22 rows

Sources: `.scratch/h4-probe/ORACLE-CELLS-2026-09-11.md` (the agent's own report, which **supersedes**
`.scratch/h4-probe/BRANCH-NUMBERS-FLOOR-R1-EVAL.md`), plus the two provisional controller-computed files
`.scratch/h4-probe/BRANCH-NUMBERS-FLOOR-R1-EVAL.md` and
`.scratch/h4-probe/CONTROLLER-INDEPENDENT-AGGREGATE-2026-09-12.md`, cited where they add or contradict something.
Conditions common to every `OR-` row unless a cell says otherwise: MODQN-harness, users 100, **cap off / segment
anchor on / interruption off** (§5 condition 5; UNKNOWN in the report, resolved 2026-09-12 from the tree); pooled ratio-of-sums Σbits/ΣJ divided once, full-buffer, no demand cap; "% vs
`A m=2dB`" against the rule **on the same set**, paired statistic = mean ± sem of the 24 per-episode relative
differences, "pp" = differences of two such percentages; consumed, per-beam max over served users; host `sat`,
pinned archive `427e6a91…` asserted in every process and recorded in every item JSON; tree
`mcrl-leo-handover-cf3`, branch `cf3/pilot-20260911`, commit `102b2d4d`, `scripts/oracle_cells.py` v1
`850f0e77…` (unfloored), v4 `7c12ae9a…` (floored evaluation), v6 `40307da6…` (floored calibration) — decision
logic identical across versions; evaluator path `StepEnvironment.evaluate_actions` (common random numbers,
nothing committed, rng deep-copied), with an exact step-0 warm-start memo; fresh env per episode; **no trained
network is touched** (the A1-OFF-s0 checkpoint `d7048d59…15cb` is read only for its `TrainerConfig`, to encode
the saved 113-dim observations); n = 24 episodes × 10 steps × 100 users per cell, ≈ 2,500 counterfactual
evaluations per step (≈ 600,000 per cell); served non-inferiority by paired cluster bootstrap over episodes
(10,000 draws, seed 20260911). Both oracles are **one-step myopic**, hence lower bounds on their mechanism's
ceiling.

### 5d. EE ceiling — budget-limited central search headroom above the simple rules (`.scratch/ee-ceiling/EE-CEILING-2026-09-11.md`) — 24 rows

Source: `.scratch/ee-ceiling/EE-CEILING-2026-09-11.md`. Conditions common to every `EC-` row unless a cell says
otherwise: MODQN-harness **with the MDP modifiers actually stated** — cap **not present in tree `102b2d4d`
(off)**, segment anchor **on** (harness default `SEGMENT_START_POWER_W`, warm start "uniform-episode-length",
**not** ablated), interruption **not present (off)**, users 100 — this family is the one that **states** them, and §5
condition 5 is what carries the same values to the other seven families; pooled ratio-of-sums Σbits/ΣJ divided once
(plain left-to-right `+=`, as the harness), full-buffer Shannon, no demand cap; consumed, per-beam max over
served users; host `sat`, pinned archive `427e6a91…` asserted in every process; tree = a `git archive` of
`/home/u24/papers/mcrl-leo-handover-cf3` @ `102b2d4d` (branch `cf3/pilot-20260911`) staged as
`sat:/home/sat/mcrl-v025-ceiling-ws/tree`, tar sha256 `922e3b3e…af2d` both ends, plus
`.scratch/ee-ceiling/scripts/`; **no learner, so bootstrap / D-2 / D-3 / z-score / penalty are n.a.**; fresh env
per episode, per-episode reseeded; evaluator `StepEnvironment.evaluate_actions` (deep-copied RNG, common random
numbers); every search is coordinate ascent with **max 3 Dinkelbach sweeps per step**, fixed user order 0..99,
strict-improvement acceptance; `[A]` rows are the evaluation set (env `9_111_000+i` / mobility `9_112_000+i`),
`[B]` rows the calibration set (`9_121_000+i` / `9_122_000+i`) and carry references and the placebo only.
Placebos on this run: RANDOM_MASKED 51,866,475.48532767 and `A m=2dB` 112,195,917.53568056 bit/J on the 24
calibration episodes, both bit-for-bit equal to the pilot premeasure (`calibration.json` sha256
`59952214…562d`). **Every search figure is a search winner, never a rule's score** (erratum 23 §3), and a lower
bound on its own one-step local optimum.

### 5e. T0 representability screen — LP-prev(1, 0) cloned onto the ratio learner's observation (`.scratch/t0-repr/T0-REPRESENTABILITY-2026-09-12.md`) — 18 rows

Source: `.scratch/t0-repr/T0-REPRESENTABILITY-2026-09-12.md`. The document declares itself as superseding **the
VERDICT-ONLY version of the same file written 2026-09-11 18:09 UTC** (a document-level supersession; no registry
row is superseded). Conditions common to every `T0R-` row unless a cell says otherwise: MODQN-harness, users 100,
**cap off / segment anchor on / interruption off** (§5 condition 5; UNKNOWN in the report, resolved 2026-09-12
from the tree), same env construction as the LP probe and
the CF3 pilot (`cf_ratio.make_env_on`, 10 steps/episode); pooled ratio-of-sums Σbits/ΣJ divided once, full-buffer
Shannon, no demand cap, **plain left-to-right float accumulation copied statement for statement from
`lp_common`, not Python's compensated `sum`**; `R_repr = (EE(clone) − EE(A m=2dB)) / (EE(T0) − EE(A m=2dB))`,
all three pooled EEs **on the same episode set**; consumed, per-beam max over served users; host `sat`, pinned
archive `427e6a91…` asserted in EVERY process; tree `mcrl-leo-handover-cf3` commit `102b2d4d` (staged
`src scripts tests` + `artifacts/PREREG-FROZEN-2026-08-25-R2.json` sha256 `8bf13e28…4543`); the ratio learner's
`TrainerConfig` is used **only** for the observation encoding and the network shape — **no learner training, no
`update()`, no gradient step on any MODQN network, no trained checkpoint loaded**; fresh env per episode, greedy
ε = 0, masked argmax, first index on ties; n = 24 episodes per set per arm (7 arms per set), 0 training seeds;
paired per-episode sem (n = 24) plus a 10,000-resample episode-cluster bootstrap (seed 20260912) for `R_repr`.

### 5f. T_SEQ representability screen (Amendment 4 §2 condition 3) — the rate-floored sequential oracle cloned onto the B2 student's observation (`.scratch/b2-representability/T-SEQ-REPRESENTABILITY-2026-09-12.md`) — 19 rows

Source: `.scratch/b2-representability/T-SEQ-REPRESENTABILITY-2026-09-12.md` ("supersedes nothing"). Conditions
common to every `TSQ-` row unless a cell says otherwise: MODQN-harness, users 100, **cap off / segment anchor on /
interruption off** (§5 condition 5; UNKNOWN in the report, resolved 2026-09-12 from the tree), the same env
construction as the oracle lane, the LP probe and the T0 screen
(`cf_ratio.make_env_on`, 10 steps/episode); pooled ratio-of-sums Σbits/ΣJ divided once, full-buffer Shannon, no
demand cap, plain left-to-right accumulation copied from `lp_common`; `R_repr = (EE(clone) − EE(A m=2dB)) /
(EE(T_SEQ) − EE(A m=2dB))`, all three pooled EEs on the **same** set; consumed, per-beam max over served users;
host `sat`, pinned archive `427e6a91…` asserted in EVERY process; tree `mcrl-leo-handover-cf3` commit `102b2d4d`,
staged tarball sha256 `12c341e5…5cba`, identical on both ends **and identical to the tree the T0 lane staged**;
the ratio learner's `TrainerConfig` is used only for the observation encoding and the network shape — **no
learner training, no `update()`, no gradient step, no trained checkpoint loaded**; teacher **T_SEQ = B-real-floor
R1** (= OR-02 / OR-05), whose pooled EE was re-derived here and whose saved joint actions were re-committed
through this report's own loop; clones = `DQNNetwork(141 → 100 → 50 → 50 → 28, tanh)`, the declared clones being
the **400-epoch** fits with epoch and τ chosen on VAL only; fresh env per episode, greedy ε = 0, masked argmax,
first index on ties; n = 24 episodes per set per arm, paired per-episode sem (n = 24) plus a 10,000-resample
episode-cluster bootstrap. **In-sample status differs by set**: the evaluation set is out-of-sample (no
evaluation episode contributed a training row, a selection decision or a hyper-parameter); the **calibration set
is in-sample by construction** — the training corpus *is* the calibration set.

### 5g. B1 credit — the difference-reward credit, the analytic lighting price, and the lighting-price oracle-first screen (`.scratch/b1-credit/B1-CREDIT-IMPLEMENTATION-2026-09-12.md`) — 23 rows

Source: `.scratch/b1-credit/B1-CREDIT-IMPLEMENTATION-2026-09-12.md`. **Host warning, unique in §5**: this family ran
on the **LOCAL laptop `LAPTOP-O8M86FNI` (WSL2)** — `nice -n 16`, `OMP_NUM_THREADS=1`, torch 1 thread, ≤ 2
concurrent processes — **with the pinned archive `427e6a91…8fe9` asserted in every process**. That is a
host/archive pair §0 trap 1 does not list (its "local" group is the *unpinned* `e07f3e1e…` archive). B1C-01 is the
tie that makes the family comparable with the sat pinned lanes: `A m=2dB` re-rolled locally reproduces the LP
probe's sat reference rolls on every published field. Other conditions common to every `B1C-` row unless a cell
says otherwise: MODQN-harness, users 100, 10-step episodes, fresh env per episode, **no MDP modifier added: cap off
/ segment anchor on / interruption off** (§5 condition 5 — the report says only "exactly as the CF3 pilot ran them"
without naming the values; the values come from the tree by controller ruling);
diagnostics are fractions over decisions (a user-step with a non-empty mask) and distributions of per-user
credits, the screen is pooled ratio-of-sums Σbits/ΣJ divided once with a full-buffer numerator, the benchmark is
wall seconds; **power accounting = consumed, per-beam (F-2): `supply(p_beam)` + `P_cir` per lit beam + `P_BB` per
lit satellite** (the report's own wording, which is the MODQN default written out); evaluation episodes env
`9,111,000+i` / mobility `9,112,000+i`, calibration `9,121,000+i` / `9,122,000+i`; rules from `cf_sources.py`, no
rule consumes RNG; code = worktree `/home/u24/papers/mcrl-leo-handover-b1`, branch
`b1/difference-reward-20260912`, engineering-core commit
`63b02dc030180b83889387b031bd1c7dff4754f5` (base `102b2d4d`). **No optimizer step, no training run, no learner
update was performed anywhere in this task.**

### 5h. E0 development training (Amendment 6 lane) — `.scratch/dev-training/E0-BATCH-1-2026-09-12.md` and `E0-FREEZE-PROVISIONAL-ALGORITHM-2026-09-12.md` — 27 rows

Sources: `.scratch/dev-training/E0-BATCH-1-2026-09-12.md` (`E0B1` below) and
`.scratch/dev-training/E0-FREEZE-PROVISIONAL-ALGORITHM-2026-09-12.md` (`E0FR`). **E1 is not curated here** — its
runs were still going.

**Provenance gap — found by Q9b, ruled on, and closed 2026-09-12.** Neither E0 document carried the PROVENANCE
block that `.scratch/curation/PROVENANCE-HEADER.md` has made mandatory since 2026-09-11, so neither stated the
physics/harness name, the MDP modifiers, the user count, the numerator convention, the power accounting, or the
DEVVAL episode seeds, and every `E0-` row first read `UNKNOWN` in those columns.
`.scratch/curation/CONTROLLER-Q9B-ADJUDICATION-2026-09-12.md` §3 supplies them — **verified by the controller from
`runs-e1-d0-d3/E0-1-D0-equal_share-k3/devval-ep00300.json` on `sat`, not from a document** — and §4 supplies the
MDP modifiers as tree properties. They have since been written into a **PROVENANCE block at the top of
`.scratch/dev-training/E0-BATCH-1-2026-09-12.md`** (added by CURATE-2, marked as retro-fitted so it is not
mistaken for the lane's own declaration), which is what the `E0-` rows below now cite. The resolved values:
**MODQN-harness; cap off / segment anchor on / interruption off; users 100; pooled ratio-of-sums Σbits/ΣJ divided
once, full-buffer Shannon, no demand cap; consumed, per-beam max over served users; host `sat`; pinned archive
`427e6a91…8fe9`; DEVVAL episode seeds (env, mobility) = `[[9211000, 9212000] … [9211023, 9212023]]`, i = 0..23,
24 episodes.** Two fields stay UNKNOWN because nothing supplies them: the D-2 / D-3 / cap-flag / z-score /
penalty tree flags, and whether a start-epoch disjointness check between DEVVAL and the DEV training triples was
ever run (the T0 lane ran one for its own sets; this lane reports none).

What the documents themselves state and every `E0-` row inherits unless a cell says otherwise: pooled EE = Σbits /
Σjoules, divided once; DEVVAL = 24 episodes, greedy, fresh env per episode, per-episode reseeded, arms sharing
every episode's epoch, population and fading stream (so the paired per-episode win counts are paired by
construction); host `sat`, `/home/sat/mcrl-v025-dev-e0-ws/`, pinned TLE
`MCRL_TLE_ROOT=/home/sat/mcrl-v025-cf3-pilot-ws/tle-pinned-427e6a91` asserted (file-set sha256
`427e6a91…8fe9`); `systemd-run --user --scope -p MemoryMax=5G`, `nice -n 10`,
OMP/MKL/OPENBLAS/NUMEXPR = 1; the formal evaluation (`9_111_000+i` / `9_112_000+i`), calibration
(`9_121_000+i` / `9_122_000+i`) and CONFIRM (`9_311_000+i` / `9_312_000+i`) episodes are untouched by every path
in this lane, enforced in code (`cf_dev.assert_dev_seed` / `assert_dev_null_key`) and by a test that records
every generator a real development run builds; DEV training triple k = 0 = `9,201,000 / 9,202,000 / 9,203,000`

**§5 revision, 2026-09-12 (post-adjudication).** Acting on
`.scratch/curation/CONTROLLER-Q9B-ADJUDICATION-2026-09-12.md`, four things changed and nothing was re-measured by
the curator:
(a) the ~110 `cap` / `segment anchor` / `interruption` `UNKNOWN` cells were **resolved** to *cap off / segment
anchor on / interruption off / users 100* — §5 condition 5, ruling §4 plus two git facts (63 row cells and 7
section preambles rewritten);
(b) the `E0-` family's provenance `UNKNOWN`s were **closed** by a PROVENANCE block retro-fitted to
`.scratch/dev-training/E0-BATCH-1-2026-09-12.md`, which all 27 `E0-` rows now cite — including the DEVVAL episode
seeds `9,211,000+i / 9,212,000+i`, i = 0..23 (ruling §3);
(c) **OR-17 moved from `CONFLICT` to `CORRECTED`** — the controller re-derived `n_moved` from the 240 raw
per-step records (unfloored 65–100, mean 82.6; floored 58–100, mean 79.9) and neither published figure was the
range; **OR-19 remains `CONFLICT`** by ruling §2, because it gates nothing and was not re-derived;
(d) two permanent annotations were attached — the **sweep-order caveat** on every row carrying the +25.35 %
B-real oracle figure (OR-02, OR-05, OR-18, TSQ-04, TSQ-16, TSQ-17; ruling §7) and
**`NOT CITABLE AS THE BASELINE (pre-S1)`** on every row carrying a pre-S1 value of the frozen checkpoint
`e6b063ef…` (FF-04, FF-05, DR-03, CS-02, CS-16, SV-MZ-01, EC-01, EC-02, E0-02; §0 trap 7, ruling §6).
§0 trap 1 was also corrected to state that **host does not imply archive**, and §0 gained trap 7.
